We provide full proofs and empirical experiments to justify the following insights: When measured by their power to effect changes in linking numbers, the layer-skipping feature in ResNets is as powerful as the attention mechanism in transformers; both ResNets and transformers are strictly more powerful than feedforward neural networks with monotonic activations, which are in turn more powerful than invertible and flow-based models; but replacing monotonic activation with a nonmonotonic one elevates a feedforward network into the same expressivity class as ResNets and transformers. These results suggest that low-dimensional topology can be a useful tool to guide designs of AI architectures. We also generalize our results from $d = 3$ to arbitrary $d > 3$.

</details>


### [74] [Review Residuals: Update-Conditioned Residual Gating for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31859)
*Kyle Kramer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Review Residuals，一种通过学习输入依赖的门控机制来缩放残差连接更新的方法，该门控同时基于当前状态和更新提议，在1B参数规模下显著优于标准残差连接和Highway门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准残差连接以固定系数1无条件地叠加每层的更新，网络在提交更新前从不评估其可靠性。受人为因素中"独立验证"原则启发，引入可学习的门控机制来条件性地缩放每次更新。

Method: 将残差连接改为 h_l = h_{l-1} + r_l * u_l，其中门控值 r_l = sigmoid(W[RMSNorm(h_{l-1}), RMSNorm(u_l)])。门控同时基于当前状态和更新提议（这是区别于先前行门控和缩放残差的关键）。

Result: 两个核心发现：(1) 深度稳定性：Highway式凸门控在20层以上出现梯度消失，而加法恒等保持形式在所有测试深度下稳定训练；(2) 随规模涌现：从60M到1B参数的多种子实验表明，小规模下无优势，但在590M时显著优于参数匹配的Highway门控和标准残差（p<0.05），在1B时优势更大，效益随模型规模增长而增加。

Conclusion: Review Residuals在深度稳定性和大规模训练方面均优于标准残差连接和高门控，且其优势随模型规模扩大而增强，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Residual connections add every sublayer's proposed update with a fixed coefficient of one; the network never evaluates whether an update is reliable before committing it. Drawing on the human-factors principle of independent verification, we introduce Review Residuals, which scale each update by a learned, input-dependent gate conditioned on both the current state and the proposed update: h_l = h_{l-1} + r_l * u_l with r_l = sigmoid(W[RMSNorm(h_{l-1}), RMSNorm(u_l)]). Conditioning the gate on the update is the property that distinguishes it from prior gated and scaled residuals. We report two findings. First, a depth-stability result: a convex (Highway-style) form of the gate reintroduces vanishing gradients and fails to train beyond ~20 layers, whereas the additive, identity-preserving form trains stably at all depths we tested. Second, an emergence-with-scale result: trained from scratch across five sizes (60M-1B parameters, multi-seed), Review Residuals show no advantage at small scale but at 590M significantly outperform both a parameter-matched Highway gate and a parameter-matched standard residual (p<0.05), with a larger advantage at 1B. The benefit grows with model size rather than shrinking.

</details>


### [75] [Sequential RC-TGAN: Generating Relational Time Series with Spectral Envelope Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31904)
*Mohamed Gueye,Yazid Attabi,Manuel Morales,Maxime Dumas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Seq. RC-TGAN，通过基于谱包络理论的损失函数和VGM离散化策略，增强生成对抗网络对关系数据库中分类与连续时间序列的周期性和季节性建模能力，并引入两个新评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成关系数据库中的时间序列（如交易日志、事件序列）常包含复杂的时序动态，但标准编码方法（如one-hot）无法捕获频域特征（如季节性和周期性）。缺乏针对关系时间序列频域保真度的鲁棒评估标准。

Method: 1) 在RC-TGAN框架中引入基于谱包络理论的可微损失函数，使生成器通过反向传播直接优化潜在周期结构；2) 采用变分高斯混合模型对连续时间序列进行离散化，将频域正则化扩展到连续数据；3) 构建以参数α控制的理论谱包络已知的模拟分类时间序列基准；4) 提出谱密度散度和谱包络散度两个新评估指标。

Result: 在真实数据集和模拟基准上，端到端方法在复现循环模式和长期季节性方面显著优于现有最先进系统，适用于分类和连续特征。

Conclusion: Seq. RC-TGAN有效提升了关系数据库中时序数据的频域建模能力，提出的评估指标为关系时间序列生成提供了更严格的评价标准。

Abstract: The generation of synthetic relational databases often involves modeling complex temporal dynamics, such as transaction logs or event sequences. A significant challenge in this domain is the handling of categorical time series (e.g., status codes), where standard encoding methods like one-hot encoding fail to capture intrinsic frequency-domain features such as seasonality and cyclicity. In this paper, we introduce Sequential RC-TGAN (Seq. RC-TGAN), a temporal extension of the RC-TGAN framework, equipped with a novel integrated loss function based on the \textit{Spectral Envelope Theory}. This differentiable loss allows the generator to directly optimize the preservation of latent periodic structures via backpropagation. While spectral envelope theory is inherently designed for categorical sequences, we extend this frequency-domain regularization to continuous time series by employing a Variational Gaussian Mixture Model (VGM) discretization strategy. To establish a mathematically rigorous evaluation standard, we simulate categorical time series governed by a parameter $α$, with exactly known theoretical spectral envelopes. Integrating these dynamic sequences into the child tables of a relational database yields a robust ground-truth benchmark for evaluating the frequency-domain fidelity of our generative framework. Furthermore, we address the lack of robust evaluation standards for relational time series by proposing two new metrics: Spectral Density Divergence and Spectral Envelope Divergence. Experimental results on real-world datasets, as well as our simulated benchmarks, demonstrate that our end-to-end approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art systems in reproducing cyclic patterns and long-term seasonality across both categorical and continuous features.

</details>


### [76] [Interface-Aware Neural Newton Preconditioning for Robust Cohesive Zone Model Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31921)
*Zhangyong Liang,Huanhuan Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经网络的界面感知牛顿预条件子（IA-NNP），用于改善隐式准静态有限元分析中内聚力模型软化导致的收敛困难问题。该方法将手工NR修正泛化为学习型、状态依赖的界面修正，仅作用于活动界面变量，不改变原始牵引-分离律。实验表明，IA-NNP能提高困难增量步的收敛性，减少收敛失败，同时保持力-位移响应不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内聚力模型在隐式准静态有限元分析中，因软化引起界面切线刚度负定、解跳变及牛顿盆地不匹配，导致先前的收敛状态成为不良初始猜测，进而引发求解停滞、错误分支收敛或反复减步长。现有方法（粘性正则化、路径跟踪、动力松弛、手工NR修改）要么改变有效响应、增加计算成本，要么依赖手工定制的界面规则，缺乏通用性和自动化程度。

Method: 提出界面感知神经牛顿预条件子（IA-NNP），包含两种实现：IA-NNP-Init用于学习型初始猜测提升，IA-NNP-NL用于迭代级非线性右预条件。该方法将手工NR修改重述为基于规则的界面提升并泛化为学习型、状态依赖的界面修正。仅作用于活动界面变量，保留原始牵引-分离律、残差组装、切线评估、历史更新和耗散检查。界面图特征编码开口位移、牵引力、切线刚度、损伤/历史变量、模态混合度、残差及邻近状态。修正量经有界、置信门控处理，仅通过原始CZM牛顿求解接受。根等价性质保证IA-NNP改变收敛路径但不改变离散CZM解集。

Result: 在水平、圆形、双界面和活动前沿等基准测试上，IA-NNP相比标准牛顿法和手工NR修改，显著提高了困难增量步的收敛性，改善了分支恢复，减少了失败次数，同时保持了力-位移响应不变。

Conclusion: IA-NNP为内聚力模型收敛难题提供了一种数据驱动的通用解决方案，避免了传统方法对有效响应的改变和手工规则依赖，具有良好的泛化能力和保持解一致性的理论保证。

Abstract: Cohesive Zone Models (CZMs) are widely used to simulate interface fracture, delamination, adhesive failure, and fiber--matrix debonding in aerospace composite structures. In implicit quasi-static finite element analyses, cohesive softening may introduce negative interface tangents, solution jumps, and Newton-basin mismatch, so the previous converged state can become a poor initial guess for the next increment. This may lead to stagnation, wrong-branch convergence, or repeated step cuts. Existing remedies, including viscous regularization, path following, dynamic relaxation, and manual Newton--Raphson (NR) modification, either alter the effective response, increase cost, or rely on hand-crafted interface rules. This work proposes an Interface-Aware Neural Newton Preconditioner (IA-NNP) for difficult CZM increments. IA-NNP recasts manual NR modification as rule-based interface lifting and generalizes it into a learned, state-dependent interface correction. The method acts only on active interface variables and preserves the original traction--separation law, residual assembly, tangent evaluation, history update, and dissipation checks. Two realizations are developed: IA-NNP-Init for learned initial-guess lifting and IA-NNP-NL for iteration-level nonlinear right preconditioning. Interface graph features encode opening, traction, tangent, damage/history variables, mode mixity, residuals, and neighboring states. The correction is bounded, confidence-gated, and accepted only through the original CZM Newton solve. A root-equivalence property shows that IA-NNP changes the path to convergence but not the discrete CZM solution set. Tests on horizontal, circular, two-interface, and active-front benchmarks show improved difficult-increment convergence, better branch recovery, and fewer failures than standard NR and manual NR modification, while preserving the force--displacement response.

</details>


### [77] [Making Sense of Touch from the Child's View for Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31943)
*Max Whitton,Zecheng Wang,Puchen Liu,Quang Tuan Truong,Shengao Wang,Manaswi Yadamreddy,Oktay Ozel,Visista Jayanti,Saniya Sekhon,Hanna Samuel Tadesse,Lawrence Miao,Junjie Wang,Jiasen Lu,Chen Yu,Boqing Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过收集26.4万个两秒长的触觉事件片段，构建了一个以婴儿为中心的触觉事件编码系统，并利用该数据集预训练发展基础模型，探究触觉在婴儿视觉概念学习中的作用及其重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究触觉是否是婴儿学习视觉概念的机制，并量化其重要性。

Method: 提出了一个结构化的婴儿触觉事件编码系统，构建了包含26.4万个两秒触觉片段的数据集，并基于该数据集预训练发展基础模型。

Result: 获得了26.4万个触觉事件片段的数据集，预训练模型揭示了触觉在婴儿学习中的重要性的初步见解。

Conclusion: 触觉在婴儿视觉概念学习中具有可量化的作用，发展基础模型为理解婴儿多模态学习提供了新视角。

Abstract: Is the sense of touch a mechanism for human babies' learning of visual concepts? If so, can we quantify its importance, and to what extent do babies rely on their sense of touch for visual learning? To approach these questions in a principled way, we propose a structured coding system for baby-centric touch events, yielding a dataset of 264k two-second clips of touch events coded according to this system. Using this dataset, we pretrain developmentally grounded models that reveal promising insights into the nature of baby learning from touch.

</details>


### [78] [Signed-Permutation Coordinate Transport for RMSNorm Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31963)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现代LLM工作流中，跨检查点传输坐标索引对象（如干预向量、稀疏自编码器、神经元集等）需要固定模型的残差流规范（gauge）。本文发现规范依赖于架构：LayerNorm残差图具有置换规范S_d（全局符号翻转），而RMSNorm具有符号置换规范B_d = S_d ⋉ {±1}^d。仅置换对齐对RMSNorm模型是对称不完整的。作者提出符号边缘化匈牙利匹配，证明原始符号相关匹配存在置换精度上限（取决于真实规范的正符号比例），符号边缘化可消除此缺陷。以坐标保持传输（而非函数级合并）为主要目标，沿着同基微调轨迹组合保存的B_d规范，在1500步时恢复91.1%的跨运行坐标（端点匹配仅60.3%），且增益不能仅通过基模型路由解释。恢复的规范能传递被置换对齐破坏的工具：TinyLlama SAE重建NMSE为0.004（B_d） vs 1.08（S_d）；Qwen情感干预保持95.8%效果 vs 17.2%；拒绝干预在S_d下符号反转；坐标保持合并行为一致。同样的协方差控制有状态训练：符号传输AdamW状态保持恢复轨迹，而置换状态遵循不同轨迹。最后，规范扫描审计表明索引级可解释性声明仅在明确规范下可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM工作流需要在不同检查点之间传输坐标索引的对象（如干预向量、稀疏自编码器、注意力归因列表、模型合并对齐等），但这些操作只有在固定了模型的残差流规范（gauge）后才是良定义的。不同架构（LayerNorm vs RMSNorm）具有不同的规范结构，现有方法忽视了这一点，导致对齐不完整。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明LayerNorm具有置换规范S_d（仅排列，全局符号翻转），RMSNorm具有符号置换规范B_d = S_d ⋉ {±1}^d。2. 提出符号边缘化匈牙利匹配算法。3. 证明原始符号相关匹配在去相关坐标下存在置换精度上限（受真实规范正符号比例约束）。4. 提出坐标保持传输方法：沿着同基微调轨迹组合保存的局部B_d规范。

Result: 1. 坐标保持传输在1500步恢复91.1%跨运行坐标（端点匹配仅60.3%）。2. TinyLlama SAE重建NMSE：B_d下0.004 vs S_d下1.08。3. Qwen情感干预效果保持：B_d下95.8% vs S_d下17.2%。4. 拒绝干预在S_d下符号反转。5. 符号传输AdamW状态保持恢复轨迹，置换状态则不同。

Conclusion: LLM工作流中的坐标索引对象传输必须考虑架构相关的规范结构。RMSNorm模型需要符号置换规范B_d而非仅置换规范S_d。符号边缘化匈牙利匹配能克服符号相关匹配的精度上限。坐标保持传输优于端点匹配。规范审计表明可解释性声明仅在明确规范下可复现，对现有LLM可解释性和对齐研究有重要方法论意义。

Abstract: Modern LLM workflows move coordinate-indexed objects across checkpoints: steering vectors, sparse autoencoders, top-$k$ neuron sets, attribution lists, and merge alignments. This is only well posed after fixing the model's residual-stream gauge, which we show is architecture-dependent: LayerNorm residual charts have permutation gauge $S_d$ (up to a global sign flip), while RMSNorm charts with generic per-channel gain have signed-permutation gauge $B_d = S_d \ltimes \{\pm 1\}^d$. Permutation-only alignment is therefore symmetry-incomplete for RMSNorm models. We introduce sign-marginalized Hungarian matching and prove a sharp failure mode: with decorrelated coordinates, raw signed-correlation matching has a structural permutation-accuracy ceiling at the positive-sign fraction of the true gauge, which sign-marginalization removes. We then make coordinate-preserving transport, not function-level merging, the primary object: composing saved-checkpoint local $B_d$ gauges along same-base fine-tuning trajectories recovers 91.1% of cross-run coordinates at 1500 steps versus 60.3% for endpoint matching, and the gain is not explained by merely routing through the base. The recovered gauge transfers tools that permutation-only alignment breaks: TinyLlama SAE reconstruction has NMSE 0.004 under $B_d$ versus 1.08 under $S_d$; Qwen sentiment steering preserves 95.8% of its effect versus 17.2%; refusal steering reverses sign under $S_d$; coordinate-preserving merges behave the same way. The same covariance governs stateful training: signed transport of AdamW state preserves the resumed trajectory, while permutation-only state follows a different one from a functionally identical checkpoint. Finally, gauge-sweep audits show index-level interpretability claims are reproducible only relative to an explicit gauge.

</details>


### [79] [Amplifying Membership Signal Through Chained Regeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31991)
*Wojciech Łapacz,Stanisław Pawlak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MADreMIA框架，通过迭代生成轨迹（链式生成）来增强生成模型的成员推断和数据集推断攻击，相比单次生成提供更丰富的成员证据信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIA和DI攻击依赖单次生成，信号弱且跨模态灵敏度有限；阴影模型训练对于大型生成模型不可行，亟需可扩展的推断方法。

Method: 利用MAD现象，通过链式迭代生成（输出作为下一次输入）构建轨迹，比较记忆样本与非成员样本在迭代过程中的连贯性和退化速率差异。

Result: 记忆训练样本在迭代再生中表现出显著更高的连贯性和更慢的退化；在图像自回归模型、扩散模型和语言模型上取得全面评估结果，并在音频模型上展示初步潜力。

Conclusion: MADreMIA是一种模型无关、无需阴影模型训练的可扩展框架，能有效增强白盒、灰盒和黑盒设置下的成员推断与数据集推断性能。

Abstract: The tendency of large generative models to memorize training data makes sample verification critical for privacy auditing and copyright enforcement. Current membership (MIA) and dataset inference (DI) attacks often rely on one-shot generations, which yield weak signals and limited sensitivity across modalities. Inspired by Model Autophagy Disorder (MAD), we introduce MADreMIA, a model-agnostic framework that enhances white-, gray-, and black-box MIA and DI. Rather than relying on shadow model training -- often infeasible for large generative models -- our framework facilitates scalable inference by leveraging inherent signals through iterative trajectories. This process utilizes chained generations across diverse modalities, where each output serves as the subsequent input, to improve membership evidence at low FPR. We demonstrate that memorized training samples exhibit significantly higher coherence and slower degradation during iterative regeneration than non-member generations. Our results show that MADreMIA provides richer signals across diverse model families and modalities; we present comprehensive evaluations for IARs, diffusion, and language models, alongside preliminary results demonstrating its potential for audio models.

</details>


### [80] [Radial Suppression Accelerates Algorithmic Generalization: A Geometric Analysis of Delayed Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32000)
*Srijan Tiwari,Aditya Chauhan,Manjot Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过几何案例分析揭示了神经网络在算法数据上先记忆后泛化（grokking）的原因：交叉熵优化导致隐藏表示径向膨胀，阻碍了结构化低维电路的发现。作者提出径向-角度分解框架，通过限制径向膨胀（将激活约束在sqrt(d)半径的超球面上）可加速泛化，在模运算任务上最高提升6倍，并在10M参数的nanoGPT上减半训练步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络为何在算法性训练数据上长时间记忆后才泛化（即grokking现象）的深层机制，并据此提出加速泛化的方法。

Method: 提出径向-角度分解分析激活空间动力学；引入单超参数范数惩罚，将激活软约束到sqrt(d)半径的超球面上，抑制径向膨胀。

Result: 在模算术任务上，该方法在MLP和Transformer上加速grokking最高达6倍；在10M参数的nanoGPT上，3位数加法训练步数减半。

Conclusion: 径向膨胀是记忆-泛化延迟的关键因素，通过约束径向分量可有效加速结构化电路的学习和泛化。

Abstract: Why do neural networks memorize algorithmic training data long before they generalize? We present a geometric case study demonstrating that, on tasks where generalization requires discovering structured low-dimensional circuits, the memorization-generalization delay is driven by radial inflation of hidden representations under cross-entropy optimization. We formalize a radial-angular decomposition of activation-space dynamics and derive three testable propositions: (i) that penalizing radial inflation induces anisotropic, data-dependent weight regularization; (ii) that it suppresses radial gradient energy below the isotropic random baseline, forcing predominantly angular updates; and (iii) that it biases convergence toward flatter minima. To empirically validate these propositions, we study a single-hyperparameter norm penalty that softly constrains activations to a sqrt(d)-radius hypersphere. On modular arithmetic, this penalty accelerates grokking up to 6x across MLPs and Transformers, and halves training steps for a 10M-parameter nanoGPT on 3-digit addition.

</details>


### [81] [Surrogate Fidelity: When Can Open LLMs Explain Closed Ones?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32008)
*Philippe Chlenski,Zachariah Carmichael,Ayush Warikoo,Chia-Tse Shao,Yingxiao Ye,Aobo Yang,Vivek Miglani,Nehal Bandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在无法获取闭源模型内部机制时，能否用开源模型作为代理进行机械可解释性分析。发现预测层面的一致性会高估归因层面的一致性，模型在答案上一致但在推理原因上常不一致。白盒信号（注意力模式等）对因果归因的预测能力弱，而黑盒输入消融反而更可靠。结论：仅凭预测层面的一致不足以支持将机械可解释性结论从开源模型迁移到闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性需要完全访问模型内部机制，但大多数部署的闭源模型仅暴露输出token的对数概率。因此需要研究：何时可以用开源模型上的测量结果来推断闭源模型的行为。

Method: 在预测、归因和表示三个层面评估代理保真度。对二分类任务，使用对数几率作为API兼容的标量读出，采用留一法归因。在Llama、Qwen、GPT和Gemini四个系列共11个模型上进行实验。

Result: 预测保真度显著高估了归因保真度；注意力模式和扰动幅度等白盒信号在模型间高度稳定，但对因果归因的预测能力弱；黑盒输入消融则能更好地捕捉因果归因。

Conclusion: 机械可解释性的结论不会自动迁移到闭源目标模型，仅靠预测层面的一致不足以支持这种迁移。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability (MI) requires full access to model internals, yet the APIs for most widely deployed language models at best expose log-probabilities over output tokens. This creates a surrogate problem: when do measurements made on open models allow us to make claims about a closed model? We evaluate surrogate fidelity at the prediction, attribution, and representation levels. For binary classification tasks, log-odds provide an API-compatible scalar readout of the model's representation space, and leave-one-out attributions provide insight into model behavior. Across eleven models spanning four families (Llama, Qwen, GPT, and Gemini), we find that prediction fidelity substantially overstates attribution fidelity: models that agree on what the answer is often disagree on why. We document an access-validity inversion: white-box signals like attention patterns and perturbation magnitudes are highly stable across models but only weakly predictive of causal attributions, which black-box input ablations capture by design. Mechanistic insight does not automatically transfer to closed targets, and prediction-level agreement is insufficient to warrant such transfer. Code and results are available at https://github.com/facebookresearch/surrogate.

</details>


### [82] [CoMet: Context and Multiplicity Decomposition for Multimodal Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32012)
*Sanghyuk Chun,William Yang,Amaya Dharmasiri,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMet是一种针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的高效不确定性估计方法，通过将不确定性分解为上下文特定项和多样性特定项，并训练轻量级后验模块来估计这些量，在开放多模态基准和幻觉检测任务上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型的不确定性来源多样（不同模态及其关系，以及开放场景下的无界答案），且现有方法（自回归生成或重复采样）效率低下。需要一种高效且能处理多源不确定性的估计方法。

Method: 将不确定性分解为上下文特定项（由给定任务或提示引起的歧义）和多样性特定项（上下文确定的多个合理答案与输入兼容的程度）。训练轻量级后验不确定性模块来估计这两项，无需自回归答案生成或重复采样。

Result: 在开放多模态基准、幻觉检测和多选VQA基准上，CoMet在保持高效性的同时，持续优于现有不确定性估计基线。

Conclusion: 通过分解不确定性来源并设计轻量后验估计模块，可以高效且有效地实现MLLMs的不确定性估计，为解决"知其不知"这一长期挑战提供了新思路。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation has been a long-standing challenge in AI models; it amounts to "knowing what you don't know," and metacognition is notoriously difficult even for humans (cf. the Dunning-Kruger effect). Although it is still far from solved even in simpler classification systems, tackling it in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is becoming increasingly important. Within MLLMs, uncertainty can stem from any of the diverse sources as well as from their relationships, and further can stem from the unbounded answers in the open-ended setting. To tackle the issues, we propose CoMet, an MLLM uncertainty estimation method by decomposing uncertainty into a context-specific term and a multiplicity-specific term. The former captures ambiguity induced by the given context (e.g., task or prompt), while the latter captures how many plausible answers determined by the context remain compatible with the given input. We train a lightweight post-hoc uncertainty module to estimate these quantities, which enables efficient uncertainty estimation without autoregressive answer generation or repeated sampling. Experiments on various open-ended multimodal benchmarks, hallucination detection, and multiple-choice visual question answering benchmarks show that CoMet consistently improves uncertainty estimation over existing baselines while remaining efficient in practice. Code is available at https://github.com/princetonvisualai/comet_uncertainty

</details>


### [83] [FedLAB: Traceable Semantic Codebooks for Federated Multimodal Graph Foundation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32016)
*Zekai Chen,Kairui Yang,Xuaner Chen,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedLAB框架，通过类型化分层码本组织多模态图知识（模态证据、节点语义、拓扑上下文），在联邦学习环境下实现可追溯语义表示，在10个基准和6个下游任务上优于基线最多7.53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图数据常分布在去中心化客户端，受隐私约束无法集中共享原始内容和局部结构。现有方法通过参数、原型、嵌入或码本交换知识，但未能显式暴露模态证据、节点语义和拓扑上下文如何共同支持预测，缺乏语义可追溯性。

Method: 提出FedLAB，构建类型化分层码本（typed hierarchical codebooks），分别编码模态证据、节点语义和拓扑上下文；通过联邦语义质心预训练（federated semantic barycenter pre-training）优化这些可追溯单元，同时保持原始多模态内容和图结构本地化。

Result: 在10个基准数据集和6个下游任务上，FedLAB相比最先进基线提升最高7.53%，并原生支持语义追溯接口。

Conclusion: FedLAB有效解决了联邦多模态图学习中表示可迁移性与语义可追溯性的矛盾，在保护隐私的同时实现了性能提升和透明推理。

Abstract: Multimodal graph foundation models aim to learn reusable knowledge from graphs enriched with text, images, attributes, and relational topology, thereby supporting diverse graph-centric and modality-centric tasks. In practice, however, such multimodal graphs are often distributed across decentralized clients, where raw contents and local structures cannot be centrally shared due to privacy constraints. This motivates federated multimodal graph foundation learning, which requires not only transferable representation learning but also intrinsic semantic traceability under strict data isolation. Existing methods usually exchange or store knowledge through parameters, prototypes, embeddings, or compact codebooks, which support optimization and transfer but do not explicitly expose how modality evidence, node semantics, and topology context jointly support predictions. To bridge this gap, we propose FedLAB, a traceable semantic codebook framework that organizes multimodal graph knowledge into typed hierarchical codebooks for modality evidence, node semantics, and topology context. FedLAB further refines these trace units through federated semantic barycenter pre-training while keeping raw multimodal contents and graph structures local. Extensive experiments on 10 benchmarks and 6 downstream tasks show that FedLAB improves over state-of-the-art baselines by up to 7.53\%, while preserving a native semantic trace interface.

</details>


### [84] [TRIAGE: Role-Typed Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32017)
*Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Hejian Sang,Xiaomin Li,Jiaxin Zhang,Xinchen Du,Zhipeng Wang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRIAGE提出了一种基于语义角色标签的信用分配框架，将动作序列分类为决定性进展、有用探索、无进展基础设施或回归，并通过固定角色条件规则为每个片段分配过程奖励，从而修正了标准GRPO中仅依赖最终结果信用分配的盲点。在ALFWorld、Search-QA和WebShop任务中，TRIAGE在成功率和交互效率上均优于GRPO及多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO对所有动作令牌使用统一的最终验证器结果作为优势，这存在两个主要盲点：在失败的轨迹中惩罚了有用的探索行为，在成功的轨迹中强化了冗余或退步的动作。需要一种更细粒度的信用分配机制来区分不同语义角色的动作片段。

Method: TRIAGE引入了一个结构化的角色分类器，将每个动作片段分为四类：决定性进展、有用探索、无进展基础设施或回归。然后通过固定的角色条件规则将这些标签映射为有界的片段级过程奖励，作为对最终验证器结果信号的补充。该方法被证明是从角色标签出发的最优片段级校正，即对每个片段的优势残差在角色变量上的投影，从而在分类器可靠时降低优势估计误差。

Result: 在ALFWorld、Search-QA和WebShop上，TRIAGE在两个策略模型上均优于GRPO，并超越了基于标量分类器的过程奖励基线和基于结果监督的共享骨干价值基线。消融实验表明，增益主要来自角色分类而非单纯增加密集奖励；成功轨迹中回归片段的可靠检测是主要贡献因素，探索信用提供一致的次要增益。在ALFWorld和WebShop的已完成轨迹中，TRIAGE分别减少10.4%和14.8%的环境交互步骤。

Conclusion: TRIAGE通过角色分级的信用分配有效修正了结果驱动强化学习中信用分配的盲点，在提升成功率的同时减少了不必要的环境交互，验证了语义角色信息在智能体信用分配中的关键价值。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning requires assigning credit to environment-facing actions such as searches, clicks, edits, navigation commands, and object interactions. Standard GRPO uses the final verifier outcome as a uniform advantage over all action tokens. This outcome signal is useful but structurally incomplete: it punishes useful exploration in failed rollouts and reinforces redundant or regressive actions in successful rollouts. We propose TRIAGE, a role-typed credit assignment framework that adds a semantic role axis to outcome credit. A structured judge classifies each segment as decisive progress, useful exploration, no-progress infrastructure, or regression, and a fixed role-conditioned rule maps these labels to bounded segment-level process rewards. This keeps verifier outcomes as the source of optimization direction while correcting the two main blind spots of outcome-only credit. We further show that role-conditioned credit is the optimal segment-level correction expressible from role labels alone -- a projection of the per-segment advantage residual onto the role variable -- so that the fixed role constants reduce advantage estimation error whenever the judge is reliable, and we connect this to lower-variance policy gradients. Across ALFWorld, Search-QA, and WebShop, TRIAGE improves success rates over GRPO for two policy models and outperforms both a scalar judge-derived process reward and an outcome-supervised shared-backbone value baseline. Ablations show that the gain comes from role typing rather than merely adding dense rewards: reliable detection of regression inside successful trajectories is the dominant contributor, while exploration credit provides a consistent secondary gain; on completed ALFWorld and WebShop rollouts, TRIAGE also reduces environment-facing turns by an additional $10.4\%$ and $14.8\%$ relative to GRPO.

</details>


### [85] [SemRF: A Semantic Reference Frame for Residual-Stream Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32022)
*Jian Gu,Aldeida Aleti,Chunyang Chen,Hongyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出语义参考系（SemRF）框架，将语义测量与残差动力学解耦，通过锚点固定、伪逆绑定和Voronoi轨迹分析，将语言模型的残差流解读为深度方向上的语义轨迹，并建立了轨迹复杂度与参数效率之间的条件性联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的残差流分析方法在中间层进行解码时，由于嵌入锚点与反嵌入读取点对所选跨度可能存在不一致，导致观测到的"运动"可能反映的是测量漂移而非真正的计算过程。需要一种能够分离语义测量与残差动力学的形式化方法。

Method: 提出语义参考系（SemRF），通过固定锚点并测量状态相对于锚点的关系来实现语义与残差动力学的解耦；采用伪逆绑定实现精确同步；利用锚点诱导语义Voronoi图，定义层间步长、贡献轮廓和不平衡诊断指标；通过Voronoi迹定义松弛管，在其内部求解最小作用路径，该路径在正二次权重下唯一且满足离散样条方程。

Result: 在受限双可逆性条件下，SemRF可提供稳定的语义基坐标、失真界和近恒等变化；最小作用路径在非空且正二次权重时唯一；低曲率意味着分段线性可压缩性和局部知识密度，低轨迹复杂度使用更少的语义自由度。

Conclusion: 通过参数到轨迹的映射，建立了轨迹复杂度与参数效率之间的条件性联系：在拟合数据的可接受参数设定中，更低作用和更低复杂度的轨迹使用更少的语义自由度。方法的保证需要受控的接口误差和显式管道约束下的小投影残差。

Abstract: Residual-stream analysis asks how language-model computation evolves across depth, but intermediate decoding requires comparable readout coordinates across layers. If embedding anchors and unembedding readout disagree on the chosen span, apparent motion may reflect measurement drift rather than computation. We introduce \emph{Semantic Reference Frames} (SemRF), an anchor-based formalism separating semantic measurement from residual dynamics. A SemRF fixes anchors and measures states against them. Pseudo-inverse tying gives exact synchronization; under restricted bi-invertibility, SemRF yields stable semantic-basis coordinates, distortion bounds, and near-identity changes. With the frame fixed, residual computation becomes a depthwise semantic trajectory. The anchors induce a semantic Voronoi diagram: distance, or evidence such as logits, assigns each layer to a coarse cell, while coordinates retain within-cell motion and margins. We define layerwise steps, contribution profiles, and imbalance diagnostics, then use the Voronoi trace to define a margin-relaxed tube. The canonical trace is the minimum-action path inside this tube; when nonempty with positive quadratic weight, it is unique and obeys a discrete spline equation away from active constraints. Excess action controls step, curvature, and profile mismatch. Low curvature implies piecewise-linear compressibility and local knowledge density: lower trace complexity means fewer semantic knots. Through the parameter-to-trajectory map, this gives a conditional link to parameter efficiency: among admissible settings fitting data, lower-action and lower-complexity traces use fewer semantic degrees of freedom. The guarantees require controlled interface error and small projection residual under explicit tube constraints.

</details>


### [86] [AdaJEPA: An Adaptive Latent World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32026)
*Ying Wang,Oumayma Bounou,Yann LeCun,Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaJEPA是一种自适应潜空间世界模型，通过在模型预测控制闭环中进行测试时自适应（每步仅需一次梯度更新），显著提升规划成功率，无需额外专家演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统潜空间世界模型在测试时冻结，当预测不准确时（尤其是在测试时分布漂移下）规划会失败，缺乏自适应能力。

Method: 提出AdaJEPA框架：训练后，在MPC闭环中执行动作块→观测下一状态作为自监督信号→更新模型→重新规划。利用观测到的状态转移作为自监督适应信号，无需专家演示。

Result: 在多种目标到达任务中，仅需每个MPC重规划步一次梯度更新，即可显著提升规划成功率。

Conclusion: 测试时自适应是提升潜空间世界模型鲁棒性的有效途径，闭环自监督更新能持续校准模型以应对分布漂移。

Abstract: Latent world models enable planning from high-dimensional observations by predicting future states in a compact latent space. However, these models are typically kept frozen at test time: when their predictions become inaccurate, planning can fail, especially under test-time distribution shift. To address this, we propose AdaJEPA, an adaptive latent world model that performs test-time adaptation within the closed loop of model predictive control (MPC). After training, AdaJEPA plans and executes the first action chunk, uses the observed next-state transition as a self-supervised adaptation signal, and replans with the updated model. This closed-loop update continuously recalibrates the world model without additional expert demonstrations. Across a range of goal-reaching tasks, AdaJEPA substantially improves planning success with as few as one gradient step per MPC replanning step.

</details>


### [87] [QVal: Cheaply Evaluating Dense Supervision Signals for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32034)
*Sergio Hernández-Gutiérrez,Matteo Merler,Ilze Amanda Auzina,Joschka Strüber,Ameya Prabhu,Matthias Bethge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QVal，一个免训练的测试平台，用于直接评估密集监督信号的质量，无需运行完整训练流程。通过对21种密集监督方法在4个环境中的基准测试，发现简单提示基线持续优于近期文献方法，且性能按方法家族聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集监督方法通常通过下游任务性能来评估，这既昂贵又混淆了信号质量与训练工程因素，且不同方法论家族因训练设置不同而难以公平比较。缺乏统一的基准来直接比较密集监督信号本身的质量。

Method: 提出QVal测试平台，核心思想是衡量密集监督信号与参考策略的Q值之间的一致性（Q-aligned）。给定状态-动作对，评估方法评分是否能正确排序动作。实验包含21种方法、4个环境、6种模型骨干，共1200+评估实验。

Result: 简单提示基线持续优于近期密集监督方法；性能按方法论家族强烈聚类；这些发现在不同模型规模、环境和观测模态下保持一致。

Conclusion: QVal提供了一种无需训练的评估方式，能有效解耦信号质量与训练工程，帮助研究者在不运行完整训练的情况下迭代改进密集监督方法。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act over long horizons, where a single trajectory can contain hundreds or thousands of actions. In these settings, outcome-only rewards provide too sparse guidance, failing to inform the model about the goodness of intermediate actions. Dense supervision methods aim to solve this problem by scoring intermediate steps, from intrinsic confidence to self-distillation and embedding similarities. However, it is common practice to evaluate them by measuring the downstream performance of a training pipeline that integrates them. This is expensive, conflates supervision quality with training engineering confounders, and renders different methodological families requiring distinct training setups incomparable. As a result, dense supervision methods are rarely benchmarked on common ground. We introduce QVal, a training-free testbed for directly evaluating dense supervision signals. Given a state-action pair, QVal measures how well a method's score is Q-aligned: whether it orders actions according to the Q-values of a strong reference-policy. This lets us compare signals before any training run and separate signal quality from other engineering choices. We instantiate QVal as QVal-v1.0, benchmarking 21 dense supervision methods across four diverse environments and seven methodological families, with over 1.2K evaluation experiments across six open-weight model backbones. We find that simple prompting baselines consistently outperform recent dense supervision methods from the literature, and that performance clusters strongly by family. These findings hold across model sizes, environments, and observation modalities. QVal is designed to be easily extensible to new environments and methods, enabling researchers to iterate on dense supervision methods before any training run.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [Protecting Futures against Silent Data Corruption -- Efficient Task Replication for Dynamic Data Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30771)
*Rüdiger Nather,Claudia Fohry,Mia Reitz*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对异步多任务（AMT）运行时系统中动态任务场景下的静默数据损坏（SDC）问题，提出了一种通过交叉验证所有对外效应来紧密耦合原始计算与副本计算的方法，并选择性重算受影响任务。在ItoyoriFBC运行时上实现，实验表明失效自由运行时间增长少于两倍，每SDC的故障纠正开销约为总运行时间的0.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着计算问题规模增长，静默数据损坏（SDC）发生概率增加。现有SDC保护方案大多假设静态任务和依赖关系，难以应对动态任务生成、任务通信、条件触碰及集群级负载均衡等动态场景。

Method: 提出一种紧密耦合原始计算与副本计算的方法，通过在与运行时系统交互时交叉验证所有对外效应（如任务产出），仅选择性重算受影响的故障任务。该方法支持运行时任务生成、C++11风格的promise/future通信、条件触碰以及基于work-first工作窃取的集群级负载均衡。

Result: 在ItoyoriFBC运行时系统上实现，使用Fibonacci和模拟H-矩阵LU分解基准测试进行实验。结果表明，尽管完全复制，失效自由运行时间增长少于两倍，主要归因于任务数量增加带来的负载均衡改善。每发生一次SDC，故障纠正开销约为总运行时间的0.5%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够以较低的额外开销有效保护AMT运行时系统中的动态任务免受SDC影响，且复制带来的负载均衡改善部分抵消了性能损失。

Abstract: As the size of computational problems grows, so does the likelihood of Silent Data Corruptions (SDCs). A common defense is replication, where the computation is repeated and correct results are determined by majority voting. Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) runtimes are generally well suited for this approach, since the inputs and outputs of task replicas can be compared, and the tasks can be recomputed if necessary. Most existing SDC protection schemes assume static tasks and dependencies. Dynamic settings are more challenging, especially in clusters, since the tasks/data must be tracked for the comparisons.
  This paper considers a particularly dynamic setting with task spawning at runtime, task communication through C++11-like promises/futures, conditional touches, and cluster-wide load balancing via work-first work stealing. We propose an approach that closely couples original and replica computations by cross-validating all outgoing effects when interacting with the runtime system. The approach selectively recomputes affected tasks only.
  We implemented the approach in the ItoyoriFBC runtime system and conducted preliminary experiments with Fibonacci and emulated $\mathcal{H}$-matrix LU decomposition benchmarks. Results show a factor of less than two increase of failure-free running times, despite full replication, which is mainly due to improved opportunities for load balancing resulting from the higher number of tasks. The overhead for failure correction was about 0.5% of the overall running time per SDC.

</details>


### [89] [StreamGuard: Low-Overhead Resilience for Real-time HPC Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30848)
*Hai Duc Nguyen,Bogdan Nicolae,Tekin Bicer,Amal Gueroudji,Matthieu Dorier,Kyle Chard,Ian Foster*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对实时科学工作流中生产者-消费者流式模式，提出了一种动态异步非阻塞检查点机制和一种进度感知的负载重分布策略，两者协同工作以在故障频发环境中维持执行进度和负载均衡，实验表明最多可将故障和性能异常的影响降低6倍，且无故障时开销低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时科学工作流运行在复杂且易出错的基础设施上，硬件故障、网络中断及资源争用导致的性能异常会严重降低性能并违反实时约束，亟需增强生产者-消费者流式模式的弹性。

Method: 提出两种互补技术：(i) 动态异步非阻塞检查点机制，在不中断计算的前提下保存进度；(ii) 进度感知的负载重分布策略，检测慢速工作者并主动重新平衡任务。

Result: 该方法将故障和性能异常的影响降低最多6倍，且在无故障执行时的开销低于1%。

Conclusion: 两种机制协同工作，能够在高错误率环境下维持前向进度和均衡执行，有效增强了实时科学工作流的弹性。

Abstract: Real-time scientific workflows operate on continuous data streams and must produce timely, high-quality results despite executing on complex, failure-prone infrastructure. Hardware faults, network disruptions, and performance anomalies caused by resource contention or system heterogeneity can severely degrade performance and violate real-time constraints. We focus on strengthening the resilience of the producer-consumer streaming pattern, a fundamental building block of scientific streaming workflows. We present two complementary techniques: (i) a dynamic, asynchronous, non-blocking checkpointing mechanism that preserves progress without interrupting computation, and (ii) a progress-aware load redistribution strategy that detects slow workers and proactively rebalances tasks. Together, these mechanisms maintain forward progress and balanced execution even in highly error-prone environments. Experimental results show that our approach reduces the impact of failures and performance anomalies by up to 6x, while introducing less than 1% overhead in failure-free execution.

</details>


### [90] [Towards Transparent Checkpointing with AI-driven Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30921)
*Hai Duc Nguyen,Tekin Bicer,Kyle Chard,Ian Foster,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文探索了使用前沿大语言模型（Claude Opus 4.7）为MPI科学应用自动生成检查点/重启（checkpoint/restart）代码的可行性。在六个不同领域的MPI应用上，LLM平均花费50分钟、消耗340万token即可生成可工作的容错代码，在五个应用上几乎零开销，且恢复效率与人工实现的方案相当。结果表明，端到端的LLM驱动弹性工程在技术上是可行的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为MPI科学应用添加可靠的检查点/重启支持是一项耗时且需要深度专业知识的专家工作，本文旨在探索能否用大语言模型端到端地自动完成这一工作，无需人工干预。

Method: 构建了一个涵盖多种领域和计算模式的MPI应用基准测试集，使用Anthropic的Claude Opus 4.7通过OpenCode CLI驱动迭代代码生成循环，为每个应用自动生成checkpoint/restart代码。

Result: 在六个科学应用中，LLM平均50分钟生成可工作的checkpoint/restart代码，消耗340万token；五个应用在正常运行中开销可忽略不计；从注入的进程故障中恢复的效率与人工实现的方案相当。

Conclusion: 自动化的端到端LLM驱动的弹性工程在技术上是可行的，适用于当前有意义的HPC应用子集。

Abstract: Adding reliable checkpoint/restart support to an MPI scientific application is a time-consuming expert effort that requires deep knowledge of both the application and resilience. We ask whether a frontier large language model can perform this work end-to-end without human intervention. We assemble a benchmark suite of MPI applications spanning diverse domains and computation patterns, and drive an iterative code-generation loop for each application using Anthropic's Claude Opus 4.7 invoked through the OpenCode CLI. Across six scientific applications, the LLM generates working checkpoint/restart code in 50 minutes on average while consuming 3.4 M tokens per application. The generated code adds negligible overhead during normal failure-free execution on five of six applications and recovers from injected process failures with efficiency comparable to human-engineered checkpoint/restart implementations. These results suggest that automated end-to-end LLM-driven resilience engineering is technically viable today for a meaningful fraction of HPC applications.

</details>


### [91] [Omni-Flow: A Unified Workflow Orchestration and Distributed KV Cache Sharing Framework for Multimodal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31093)
*Bin Xiao,Jingfu Dong,Changran Wang,Yitian Chen,Xiaoyu Zhao,Yuqi Peng,Jianping Lin,Yuchen Xie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Omni-Flow是一个面向多模态推理的分布式调度框架，通过控制流、数据流和计算流三层抽象，统一编排异构计算单元、高效传输中间数据并共享KV缓存与模型权重，支持多种异构场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方案将LLM和扩散模型独立部署，缺乏面向多模态流水线的系统级抽象，导致编排逻辑分散、传输路径与特定模型紧耦合、集成新模型成本高。

Method: 提出三层抽象架构：控制流层通过Python DSL定义工作流，将异构单元编排为统一数据流图；数据流层提供分布式KV缓存抽象，实现三级分页存储（GPU/CPU/SSD）上的零拷贝跨角色传输；计算流层支持多轮对话中的复杂多模态前缀匹配KV复用，通过统一SGLang接口让扩散模型直接复用LLM前向路径。

Result: 支持全模态对话（LongCat-Next）和复杂图像生成流水线（HunyuanImage-3）等多种异构场景，提供一致的编程模型。

Conclusion: Omni-Flow通过三层抽象有效解决了多模态推理中的编排、传输和共享三大挑战，为异构多模态推理提供了统一的系统级解决方案。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) inference evolves from text-only to multimodal paradigms, inference systems face three challenges: (1) flexible orchestration of multimodal workflows, where heterogeneous computing units exhibit complex dependencies and concurrent control; (2) efficient transmission of massive intermediate data across processes and nodes, with tensors flowing at high speed among heterogeneous roles; and (3) efficient sharing of KV caches and model weights across roles to eliminate redundant GPU memory. Existing solutions deploy LLMs and diffusion models independently, lacking a system-level abstraction for multimodal pipelines; this scatters orchestration logic, tightly couples transmission paths to specific models, and incurs high cost to integrate new models. To address these challenges, we present Omni-Flow, a distributed scheduling framework for multimodal inference through a three-layer abstraction. The Control Flow layer defines workflows via a Python DSL, orchestrating heterogeneous units into a unified dataflow graph that supports static DAGs and dynamic routing, with built-in service discovery and diverse load-balancing strategies. The Data Flow layer provides a distributed KV cache abstraction beyond prefill/decode separation, unifying allocation and enabling direct cross-role transmission across a three-tier paged storage hierarchy (GPU/CPU/SSD) over zero-copy, low-latency channels. The Compute Flow layer supports complex multimodal prefix matching for KV reuse across multi-turn dialogues, and takes over KV cache and sampling logic via a unified SGLang interface, letting diffusion models directly reuse the LLM forward path under unified parallel semantics. We demonstrate that Omni-Flow supports diverse heterogeneous scenarios with a consistent programming model, including omni-modal dialogue (LongCat-Next) and complex image generation pipelines (HunyuanImage-3).

</details>


### [92] [AC$^2$P$^2$SL: Adaptive Communication-Computation Pipeline Parallel Split Learning over Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31276)
*Chenyu Liu,Zhaoyang Zhang,Zirui Chen,Zhaohui Yang,Chunhui Feng,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AC²P²SL框架，通过自适应通信-计算流水线并行拆分学习，在无线边缘网络中实现多微批次细粒度流水线并行，有效重叠通信与计算，显著降低训练延迟，并通过联合优化问题及自适应重分配策略增强系统鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拆分学习中计算与通信过程顺序执行导致训练时间长，现有方法未充分考虑无线边缘网络中用户设备异构性及动态环境约束。

Method: 提出AC²P²SL框架：1) 将UE与BS的通信和计算过程构建为统一流水线，实现多微批次细粒度流水线并行；2) 考虑通信、计算、存储三维度系统约束及UE异构性，构建联合优化问题最小化训练时间；3) 设计拆分与预分配算法提升流水线效率；4) 设计自适应重分配策略应对动态环境变化。

Result: 大量实验证明AC²P²SL在降低训练时间方面具有有效性和鲁棒性，同时保障数据隐私。

Conclusion: AC²P²SL通过流水线并行机制显著减少拆分学习训练延迟，支持UE异构性，适应动态环境，为无线边缘网络中的高效协同学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In wireless edge networks, split learning (SL) enables base station (BS) to utilize the distributed data and computing power across user equipments (UEs) to achieve collaborative model training while protecting local data privacy. However, the inherent sequential execution of computation and communication processes in conventional SL usually leads to long training times. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes an adaptive communication-computation pipeline parallel split learning (AC$^2$P$^2$SL) framework. By conceptualizing the communication and computation processes of UEs and the BS as a unified pipeline, AC$^2$P$^2$SL achieves fine-grained pipeline parallelism across multiple micro-batches. Through this approach, effective overlapping of communication and computation is achieved which results in significant reduction of the overall training latency. Moreover, by considering the system constraints in the communication, computation, and storage dimensions as well as the heterogeneity of UEs, we formulate a joint optimization problem to minimize the training time and propose a corresponding split and pre-allocation algorithm to further enhance the pipeline efficiency. Additionally, accounting for the practical dynamic environments for the UEs, we design an adaptive re-allocation strategy to enhance the system resilience. Extensive experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of AC$^2$P$^2$SL in reducing training time while ensuring data privacy preservation.

</details>


### [93] [An Empirical Analysis of High-Performance Computing Education in Germany](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31300)
*Anna-Lena Roth,Jonas Posner*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 通过对德国102所高校HPC教育的系统评估，发现67.6%的院校开设HPC课程但主要集中在硕士选修阶段，61.8%的院校拥有HPC集群但仅23%明确用于教学；教学访问限制与实践中实践能力培养不足存在显著关联，揭示了德国高等教育中HPC理论与实践的结构性失衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管HPC日益重要，但跨机构层面缺乏关于HPC教育如何实施、课程如何与本地基础设施关联的系统性实证证据。现有空白限制了课程设计的优化和教学资源的有效配置。

Method: 对德国102所高校的模块手册和课程目录进行系统实证评估，识别178门HPC相关课程，评估其能力覆盖范围和课程定位；同时评估各高校HPC集群基础设施的可用性、规模及教学可访问性记录；采用统计分析检验基础设施访问限制与实践能力培养之间的关联。

Result: 67.6%的院校提供至少一门HPC课程，但以硕士选修为主，本科阶段整合有限；61.8%的院校运营HPC集群，仅23.0%明确记录可用于教学（基础设施主要服务于研究）；统计显著关联表明教学访问限制与课程中资源管理、集群使用、并行调试和性能分析等实践能力培养不足相关。

Conclusion: 德国高等教育中HPC教育存在理论教学与实践能力培养之间的结构性失衡，主要源于HPC基础设施对教学的可访问性不足，限制了学生实践技能的培养。

Abstract: The growing importance of High-Performance Computing (HPC) requires the systematic integration of parallel programming and performance-oriented competencies into computational science curricula. Effective HPC education combines theoretical foundations with practical experience on real cluster infrastructures, enabling students to understand scalability, efficiency, and architectural differences between shared and distributed memory systems. However, cross-institutional evidence on how HPC education is implemented, and how curricula relate to locally available infrastructure, remains limited. We address this gap through a systematic empirical assessment of HPC education at 102 academic institutions in Germany. Based on module handbooks and course catalogs, we identified 178 HPC-related courses and evaluated their competency coverage and curricular placement. We additionally assessed local academic HPC cluster infrastructures with respect to availability, size, and documented accessibility for teaching. The results show that 67.6% of institutions offer at least one HPC-related course, but these offerings are predominantly elective modules at the master's level, with limited integration in bachelor's programs. Although 61.8% of institutions operate HPC clusters, only 23.0% explicitly document their availability for educational use, as infrastructures are mainly reserved for research. Statistical analysis indicates a significant association between restricted teaching access and reduced curricular emphasis on practical competencies such as resource management, cluster usage, parallel debugging, and performance analysis. Overall, the findings reveal a structural imbalance between theoretical instruction and the development of practical HPC competencies in German higher education.

</details>


### [94] [Performance Analysis in Parallel Programming Education: A Comparative Usability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31458)
*Anna-Lena Roth,David James,Jonas Posner,Michael Kuhn*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了EduMPI，一个为教学设计的MPI并行程序性能分析工具，通过简化集群使用和提供直观的可视化界面，帮助学生理解并行程序性能，降低学习门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的性能分析工具虽然功能强大，但复杂性高，需要掌握集群架构、资源管理、MPI等多方面知识，难以在教学中有效整合，学生入门门槛过高。

Method: 开发了EduMPI工具，提供直观的GUI界面，自动化集群上的程序执行，并实时可视化MPI通信过程，让学生能够追踪进程通信及其在集群中的物理位置，交互式地发现性能问题。

Result: 通过用户研究对比EduMPI与专业性能分析工具，证明EduMPI能够降低入门门槛，帮助学生直观理解并行程序性能，提升了教学价值。

Conclusion: EduMPI作为教学辅助工具，有效克服了现有性能分析工具在教育场景中的使用障碍，有助于学生在并行编程课程中更好地学习MPI编程和性能优化。

Abstract: Parallel programming curricula encompass not only the development of parallel code and algorithm design but also emphasize efficiency, optimization, and performance analysis. To equip students with the skills necessary for writing efficient parallel code using message passing with MPI, practical experience on HPC environments is essential. Performance analysis tools assist in identifying issues such as load imbalances or bottlenecks. Despite their use by experienced developers, these tools' complexity and required knowledge of cluster architectures, resource management, MPI, and common parallel issues hinder their educational integration. To address these barriers, we developed EduMPI, a learning support tool designed to simplify cluster usage and performance analysis for students. EduMPI offers an intuitive GUI that automates program execution on clusters and delivers near-real-time visualizations of MPI communication. This enables students to track process communication according to their physical placement within the cluster and detect performance problems interactively. This paper presents a user study comparing EduMPI with established professional performance analysis tools, demonstrating that EduMPI lowers entry barriers and fosters an intuitive understanding of parallel program performance, thereby enhancing its educational value.

</details>


### [95] [LASER: Load-Aware Serving with Early-Exit for Reasoning LLMs at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31580)
*Zhiqing Tang,Size Li,Hanshuai Cui,Zilan Huang,Jianxiong Guo,Tian Wang,Yuan Wu,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LASER是一种面向边缘部署的负载感知服务框架，通过自适应早期退出阈值和难度感知的推理预算预分配，在保持高推理质量的同时显著降低大推理模型的长思维链推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型(如DeepSeek-R1)在边缘设备部署时，长思维链序列与受限资源之间存在冲突。现有基于置信度的早期退出方法使用固定阈值，忽略了多请求并发和负载波动问题。

Method: 提出LASER框架，包含两个互补设计：(1)负载感知的自适应退出阈值，根据实时系统负载在经验验证的鲁棒范围内调整置信度阈值；(2)难度和负载感知的推理预算预分配，根据请求难度和系统容量分配计算资源。将问题建模为推理质量与服务延迟的联合优化。

Result: 在两种推理模型、四个基准测试和多种负载条件下的实验表明：平均延迟降低17-38%，服务级别目标满意度提升3-6%，平均准确率仅损失1%。

Conclusion: LASER通过负载感知的早期退出策略有效平衡了边缘部署中推理质量与服务延迟，在几乎不牺牲准确率的前提下显著提升了服务效率。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) such as DeepSeek-R1 have achieved strong performance through extended chain-of-thought (CoT) generation. However, deploying them on edge devices raises a conflict between long CoT sequences and constrained resources. Recent confidence-based early exit methods reduce CoT length for individual requests, yet they apply fixed thresholds from a single-request perspective, ignoring multi-request concurrency and load fluctuation in edge serving. To bridge this gap, we propose \underline{L}oad-\underline{A}ware \underline{S}erving with \underline{E}arly-exit for \underline{R}easoning (LASER). LASER couples two complementary designs: (1) a load-aware adaptive exit threshold that adjusts the confidence bar based on real-time system load within an empirically validated robust range, and (2) a difficulty- and load-aware reasoning budget pre-allocation that assigns compute resources by request difficulty and system capacity. We formulate the problem as a joint optimization of reasoning quality and service latency. Experiments on two reasoning models, four benchmarks, and diverse load conditions show that LASER reduces average latency by 17--38\% and improves service-level objective (SLO) satisfaction by 3--6\% over fixed-threshold baselines, at an average accuracy cost of only 1\%.

</details>


### [96] [Decoupling Trust in Byzantine CRDTs: Fine-grained Post-Compromise Handling without Breaking Causality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31759)
*Amos Brocco*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将身份信任与内容信任解耦的细粒度信任模型，通过确定性重构，在保留已接受更新的同时，允许基于身份和更新语义选择性包含或排除更新，从而在拜占庭环境下实现鲁棒且灵活的CRDT系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CRDT假设参与者是良性的，但在拜占庭环境中，基于身份过滤的收敛方法在攻击后场景中失效——无法追溯排除恶意参与者先前提交的更新，且可能破坏因果依赖关系。

Method: 将身份信任与内容信任解耦，引入融合两个维度的细粒度信任模型；基于确定性重构，允许副本保留已接受的更新，同时基于发起者身份（如公钥）和更新语义进行选择性包含/排除；信任决策可整合应用层策略。

Result: 该方法保持了因果一致性，能够在去中心化CRDT系统中鲁棒且灵活地处理拜占庭行为和故障行为。

Conclusion: 该细粒度信任模型为后攻击场景下的CRDT系统提供了更强的安全性，在不破坏因果依赖的前提下实现了灵活的可追溯信任管理。

Abstract: Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs) provide strong eventual consistency without coordination, but classical approaches assume benign participants. In Byzantine settings, convergence is typically enforced through agreement on update validity, often relying on identity-based filtering. However, such approaches struggle in post-compromise scenarios, where a previously correct participant becomes malicious: retroactive exclusion of its updates may break causal dependencies and invalidate subsequent computations. In this paper, we decouple identity-based trust from content-based trust and introduce a fine-grained trust model that combines both dimensions. Building on deterministic reconstruction, our approach allows replicas to preserve previously accepted updates while enabling selective inclusion or exclusion based on both the originating identity (e.g., public keys) and the semantics of individual updates. Trust decisions can incorporate application-level policies, enabling precise control over the impact of each update on the system state. Our approach preserves causal consistency and enables robust and flexible handling of both Byzantine and faulty behavior in decentralized CRDT systems.

</details>

### [97] [What Drives Interactive Improvement from Feedback?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30774)
*Bartłomiej Cupiał,Jan Łojek,Mikołaj Garstecki,Szymon Pobłocki,Alicja Ziarko,Piotr Miłoś*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过受控的学生-教师评估协议，研究了多轮语言智能体交互中自然语言反馈的真正效用，发现自我生成反馈的提升有限，而外部强教师能带来显著反馈特异性增益，且学生利用反馈的能力比教师身份更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作中多轮交互的准确率提升可能源于重采样、格式修正或额外测试时计算等混淆因素，而非真正的反馈利用。作者旨在分离这些效应，量化自然语言反馈相对于简单重复尝试的净增益。

Method: 构建受控学生-教师协议，在Omni-MATH、Codeforces、BBEH Linguini和ARC-AGI1四个基准上，使用13个开源模型（同时作为学生和教师），系统比较外部反馈、自我反馈和无引导自精炼三种设置，并控制交互历史、任务难度和教师对特权信息的访问。

Result: 多轮改进往往不能证明反馈被真正利用；自我生成反馈相比无引导自精炼几乎没有额外增益；最强外部教师能产生大幅反馈特异性增益；学生-教师交互矩阵显示增益更多由学生利用反馈的能力驱动，而非教师身份，但固定学生时教师选择依然重要。

Conclusion: 基于反馈的智能体应以重复尝试为基线进行评估；反馈利用能力（而非反馈可用性）是交互式改进的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: We study when natural-language feedback produces improvement beyond the gains obtainable from repeated attempts alone. In multi-turn language agent setting, higher final accuracy can reflect useful feedback, but it can also arise from resampling, format correction, or additional test-time computation. To separate these effects, we introduce a controlled student-teacher protocol across Omni-MATH, Codeforces, BBEH Linguini, and ARC-AGI1, evaluating thirteen open-weight models in both student and teacher roles. We compare external feedback, self-feedback, and unguided self-refinement, while varying interaction history, task difficulty, and teacher access to privileged task information. Across settings, we find that multi-turn improvement is often not evidence of feedback use: self-generated feedback adds little beyond unguided self-refinement, whereas the strongest external teachers produce substantially larger feedback-specific gains, suggesting that useful feedback must provide guidance beyond generic retry. Dense student-teacher interaction matrices further show that interactive gains are driven more by the student's ability to use feedback than by the teacher's identity, although teacher choice remains important for a fixed student. These results suggest that feedback-based agents should be evaluated against repeated-attempt baselines, and that ability to act on feedback, not merely feedback availability, is a central bottleneck for interactive improvement. We release our controlled student-teacher evaluation framework at https://j-lojek.github.io/feedback-generation-is-a-bottleneck/.

</details>


### [98] [Contrastive Reflection for Iterative Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30840)
*Derek Koh,Jinghui Mo,Benjamin H. Le,Jiening Zhan,Baofen Zheng,Kevin Bevis,Nathaniel C. Owen,Lauren Elizabeth Charney,Wenqiong Liu,Jingwei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出对比反思框架，利用错误锚定的行为切片和邻近成功示例，通过教师LLM生成针对性提示编辑，并在验证集上迭代优化检索增强QA agent的性能，在HotpotQA上将准确率从51.4%提升至60.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent在信息检索中的应用日益广泛（检索查询、答案合成、IR评估），但其提示优化在应用场景中更像调试而非盲目搜索。工程师需要知道哪些行为失败、哪些邻近行为成功、两者差异何在，以及编辑是否提升保留集质量而不引入回归。现有方法缺乏这种可检查的对比分析机制。

Method: 提出Contrastive Reflection迭代提示优化框架：1）从任务级质量定义出发，QA agent暴露检索/推理轨迹，评分agent暴露维度级分数和理由；2）基于这些结构化轨迹识别错误锚定的行为切片；3）从同一区域添加邻近成功示例；4）让教师LLM提出针对性提示编辑；5）仅在验证性能提升且通过回归检查时接受候选编辑。框架使用基于树的切片选择器进行实例化。

Result: 在HotpotQA检索增强QA任务上，一次树选择的对比修复将保留集精确匹配准确率从51.4%提升至60.4%。仅使用失败示例或随机证据的变体改进较小且会破坏更多先前正确的示例。轻量级指令对比实验显示，方法接近现代提示优化器：MIPROv2达到59.4%，GEPA达到57.0%。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一个可解释的IR agent优化循环，使提示修复更具可检查性和验证驱动性，是迈向结构化、对比式的prompt调试而非盲目搜索的重要一步。

Abstract: LLM agents are becoming central to information retrieval: they issue retrieval queries, synthesize answers, and increasingly serve as judges for IR evaluation. Improving the prompts that control these agents is an optimization problem, but in applied IR settings it often looks less like blind search and more like debugging. Engineers need to know which behavior failed, which nearby behavior still worked, what distinguishes the two, and whether a prompt edit improves held-out quality without introducing regressions.
  We present Contrastive Reflection, an iterative prompt-optimization framework for agentic IR workflows. The framework starts from a task-centric quality definition: QA agents expose retrieval or reasoning traces, and grading agents expose dimension-level scores and rationales. These structured traces are used to identify error-anchored behavioral slices, add nearby successful examples from the same region, and ask a Teacher LLM to propose a targeted prompt edit. Candidate edits are accepted only when validation performance improves, optionally subject to regression checks. We instantiate the framework with a tree-based slice selector, but the contribution is the contrastive reflection loop rather than the tree itself.
  On a public HotpotQA retrieval-augmented QA setup, one tree-selected contrastive repair improves held-out exact-match accuracy from 51.4% to 60.4%. Failure-only and random-evidence variants improve less and break more previously correct examples. A light instruction-only comparison places the method near modern prompt optimizers: MIPROv2 reaches 59.4% and GEPA 57.0%. The result is an interpretable optimization loop for IR agents, aimed at making prompt repair more inspectable and validation-driven.

</details>


### [99] [How Can AI Find My Model? A Model-Finding Experimental Study Considering Data Formats, Embeddings, and Retrieval Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30846)
*Jhon G. Botello,Jose J. Padilla,Erika Frydenlund,Krzysztof Rechowicz,Eric Weisel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了数据表示、Transformer嵌入模型和检索策略对基于自然语言查询的仿真模型发现性能的影响，结果表明数据表示至关重要，开源嵌入模型表现优异，且重排序方法对复杂查询尤为重要，为AI驱动的模型发现、可组合性和互操作性提供了基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真模型复用时，从大量共存模型中识别出符合建模意图的模型仍然困难，亟需利用AI检索方法在语义层面解决这一挑战。

Method: 通过实验研究，系统评估了不同数据表示方式、Transformer-based嵌入模型以及检索策略对自然语言查询下仿真模型发现效果的影响，并使用recall@5和nDCG@5等标准信息检索指标进行评测。

Result: 数据表示方式对检索性能有显著影响；开源嵌入模型能够达到高性能；重排序方法在查询复杂度增加时尤为重要。

Conclusion: 该工作为AI驱动的模型发现建立了基线，并讨论了其在推动AI驱动的可组合性和互操作性方面的作用。

Abstract: Discovering simulation models for reuse remains a fundamental challenge in Modeling and Simulation (M&S). When many models coexist, identifying those that align with a given modeling intent remains difficult. Recent advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI), particularly retrieval-based approaches, offer a promising pathway to operate at this semantic layer. In this paper, we present an experimental study investigating the impact of data representation, transformer-based embedding models, and retrieval strategies on the discovery of simulation models using natural language queries. We evaluated performance across multiple query types using standard information retrieval metrics, including recall@5 and nDCG@5. Results show that data representation matters, open-source embedding models can achieve high performance, and reranking methods are important, especially as query complexity increases. This work provides a baseline for AI-driven model discovery and discusses its role in advancing toward AI-driven composability and interoperability.

</details>


### [100] [BayesBench: Evaluating LLM Belief Trajectories Under Multi-Turn Evidence Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30850)
*Ankur Samanta,Akshayaa Magesh,Tal Lancewicki,Ayush Jain,Youliang Yu,Paul Sajda,Kaveh Hassani,Aditya Modi,Daniel R. Jiang,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BayesBench基准，评估LLM在多轮对话中是否像贝叶斯理性推理者一样更新信念。测试7个模型（3B-70B）发现：规模提升有助于潜在变量推理和证据累积，但推理能力并未可靠地转化为下游预测任务，暴露出推断与理性信念更新之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅以单轮格式评分模型最终回答，忽略了多轮对话中信念更新的过程。本文旨在探究LLM在多轮环境中的信念更新是否与理性贝叶斯推理者一致。

Method: 构建BayesBench基准，包含三个渐进复杂的仿真环境：(i)贝叶斯估计——模型从序列证据中推断未知参数；(ii)贝叶斯预测——将推断的潜在变量信念转化为结果预测；(iii)潜在框架贝叶斯预测——观测通过用户角色框架过滤，需联合推断潜在状态和角色。测试7个LLM（3B-70B）。

Result: 模型规模提升有助于潜在变量推断和证据累积，有时更新可匹配贝叶斯后验；但这些提升并未可靠地迁移到下游预测任务，暴露出推断潜在结构与理性更新目标结果信念之间的差距。

Conclusion: LLM在多轮推理中能部分实现贝叶斯信念更新，但从推断到理性预测的迁移存在系统性缺陷，需进一步研究如何弥合这一差距。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically deployed in multi-turn conversations, where each turn provides new evidence that should reduce epistemic uncertainty about their environment. Acting rationally then requires inferring the unobserved quantities that govern it and updating beliefs about them as evidence accumulates. Yet most evaluations only score the model's final-turn answer in a single-turn format, leaving this process unexamined. We ask how closely LLMs' belief updates match those of a rational Bayesian reasoner in multi-turn settings, and introduce BayesBench, a suite of simulation environments that probe this across three progressively complex tasks: (i) Bayesian estimation, where the model infers an unknown parameter from sequential evidence; (ii) Bayesian prediction, where the model turns inferred beliefs about a latent variable into outcome forecasts; and (iii) latent-framed Bayesian prediction, where observations are filtered through a user-persona framing, requiring joint inference over the latent state and the persona. Across seven LLMs (3B--70B), scaling improves latent inference and evidence accumulation, with updates occasionally matching the Bayesian posterior. However, these gains do not reliably carry over to downstream prediction, exposing a gap between inferring latent structure and using it to rationally update beliefs about the target outcome.

</details>


### [101] [When Does Learning to Stop Help? A Cost-Aware Study of Early Exits in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30852)
*Zhe Dong,Fang Qin,Manish Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究推理语言模型的早期停止策略，提出LearnStop方法，通过探测短答案和在线特征（如置信度、熵、投票份额等）在固定预算检查点判断是否停止。实验发现：学习式停止在自由形式数学任务上优于固定预算前沿和标量退出策略（GSM8K上增益+0.028），但在多项选择和极难任务上标量规则更具竞争力。结论：学习式停止不是标量退出的通用替代，而是一种依赖于轨迹结构的工具，适用于问题在预算前正确但缺乏可靠单一标量信号的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在不同实例上的计算量存在差异，但尚不清楚学习式停止规则何时能胜过简单的置信度或收敛阈值。现有方法缺乏对何时学习停止真正有益的清晰边界。

Method: 提出LearnStop，一种免隐状态的检查点停止器，在固定预算检查点从当前推理前缀中探测短答案，并利用在线特征（答案置信度、熵、前缀投票份额、答案稳定性和回溯标记密度）预测前缀正确性。

Result: 在18个任务-模型设置上，自由形式数学任务中学习式多特征停止改进了固定预算前沿并优于标量退出（GSM8K上适配增益+0.157，配对增益+0.028）；多项选择和极难任务中标量规则更具竞争力。

Conclusion: 学习式停止不是标量退出的通用替代，其价值取决于轨迹结构。当许多问题在完整预算前正确但缺乏可靠单一标量信号时，学习停止有用；当置信度或答案收敛已解决停止问题时，其收益基本消失。

Abstract: Reasoning models spend different amounts of useful computation across instances, but it remains unclear when a learned stopping rule improves over simple confidence or convergence thresholds. We study this question with LearnStop, a hidden-state-free checkpoint stopper for reasoning language models. At fixed budget checkpoints, LearnStop probes a short answer from the current reasoning prefix and predicts prefix correctness from online features such as answer confidence, entropy, prefix vote share, answer stability, and backtracking-marker density. Across 18 task-model settings spanning GSM8K, MATH-500, MMLU-Pro, AIME-90, GPQA, Qwen3, and DeepSeek-R1 distillations, the answer is task-dependent. On free-form math, learned multi-feature stopping improves the fixed-budget frontier and often beats scalar exits: on GSM8K with Qwen3-32B, the empirical frontier reaches a post-hoc peak adapt gain of +0.157, validation-selected operating points preserve positive gains, and the paired gain over the strongest scalar baseline is +0.028. On multiple-choice and very hard settings, scalar confidence, entropy, or stability rules are competitive or stronger. We therefore frame learned stopping not as a universal replacement for scalar exits, but as a tool whose value depends on trajectory structure. We further provide validation-selected operating points, paired bootstrap tests, finite-grid lost-correct risk calibration, cost accounting under KV-fork, prefix-cache, and black-box regimes, H100 serving profiles, checkpoint-schedule sweeps, transfer analyses, and robustness checks. The main practical finding is that learned stopping is useful when many questions become correct before full budget but do not exhibit a single reliable scalar stopping signal; its benefits largely disappear when confidence or answer convergence already solves the stopping problem.

</details>


### [102] [Beyond expert users: agents should help users construct preferences, not just elicit them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30863)
*Irena Saracay,Ludwig Schmidt,Carlos Guestrin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出传统智能体假设用户拥有完善偏好是不现实的，提出了基于信息经济学的CoPref偏好构建模型，并构建了CoShop交互基准测试，发现前沿模型在帮助用户获取领域知识方面表现不佳，准确率不超过56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能体系统假设用户拥有完善且明确表达的偏好，当任务不明确时默认提出澄清问题。然而，用户往往缺乏形成偏好所需的领域知识，直接询问偏好是无效的。本文旨在形式化用户如何通过智能体的对话行为逐步构建偏好的过程。

Method: 借鉴信息经济学中的搜索-体验-信任（Search-Experience-Credence）框架，提出CoPref模型来形式化用户基于智能体对话行为构建偏好的过程。基于此构建CoShop交互式基准测试，模拟智能体与CoPref用户对话并做出推荐，测试智能体能否帮助用户获取必要知识来明确任务。

Result: 评估了五种前沿模型，发现在CoShop上即使经过五轮交互，所有智能体的准确率均不超过56%。失败的主要原因不在于智能体查找物品的能力，而在于交互过程未能有效扩展用户对自身需求的认识。

Conclusion: 当前智能体系统在帮助用户构建偏好、获取领域知识方面存在严重缺陷，单纯依靠澄清问题不足以应对用户偏好不完善的情况，需要设计更有效的交互策略来辅助用户学习。

Abstract: Agents typically assume an expert user -- one with well-formed preferences about what they want -- and default to clarifying questions whenever the task is underspecified. We argue this assumption is unrealistic. Users often lack the domain knowledge to have completely specified preferences; if asked about their preference on some feature, the user may be unable to answer without the agent helping the user to learn some domain knowledge needed to form a preference for that feature, e.g., via examples or explanations. To formalize these principles, we draw on the Search-Experience-Credence framework from Information Economics to introduce CoPref, a model of how users construct preferences based on agent dialog actions. We then study these ideas concretely in agentic recommender systems, proposing CoShop, an interactive benchmark. In CoShop, an agent converses with and makes recommendations for a CoPref user. The agent's performance depends on whether it can help the user gain the knowledge needed to specify the task well. Evaluating five frontier models, we find that no agent exceeds 56% accuracy on CoShop despite five turns of interaction. Failures stem not from agents' ability to find items, but from how little the interaction expands what users know about what they want.

</details>


### [103] [Investigating Multi-Agent Deliberation in Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30906)
*Cor Steging,Ludi van Leeuwen,Tadeusz Zbiegień*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索了基于大语言模型的多智能体 deliberation 方法在法律推理任务中的应用，提出了两种受法庭程序和法律论证启发的新型多智能体框架。实验表明，多智能体框架与基线模型整体性能相当，但产生显著不同的答案，能够成功解决基线模型无法解决的案例，尤其在需要多角度批判性思考的问题上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域中多智能体方法尚未被充分探索，而基于LLM的智能体在法律推理中的潜力值得挖掘。同时，多智能体 deliberation 方法可能提供单一大模型所不具备的多样化推理能力。

Method: 提出两种受法庭程序和法律论证启发的多智能体 deliberation 框架，在法律和非法律基准数据集上进行实验，与基线大语言模型进行对比，并进行定性评估。

Result: 多智能体框架与基线模型整体性能相当，但产生显著不同的答案；能解决基线模型无法解决的案例，反之亦然；在需要多角度批判性思考的问题上表现更优。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统是AI在法律领域的一个有前景的方向，受法律启发的多智能体 deliberation 方法具有潜力。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence is increasingly applied to the field of law, and has the potential to increase access to justice. One particular movement that is gaining traction is that of agentic AI, wherein AI agents, based on Large Language Models (LLMs) can take autonomous actions. In particular, multi-agent approaches in the legal domain remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we investigate multi-agent deliberation methods for legal reasoning tasks using LLMs. We explore multi-agent deliberation (MAD) and introduce two novel multi-agent frameworks inspired by courtroom procedures and legal argumentation. Our experiments on both legal and non-legal benchmarks reveal that multi-agent frameworks achieve comparable overall performance to baseline large language models, but produce significantly distinct answers. Notably, these approaches can successfully solve cases that the baseline fails to address, and vice versa. We conduct a qualitative evaluation and highlight scenarios where multi-agent frameworks outperform monolithic approaches. For example, multi-agent approaches appear better suited for answering questions that require critical thinking from multiple perspectives. Our work positions multi-agent systems as a promising direction for AI in the legal domain, while demonstrating the potential of law-inspired multi-agent approaches for deliberation.

</details>


### [104] [Why Solve It Twice? Hierarchical Accumulation of Skills for Transfer-Efficient ML Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30911)
*Yongbin Kim,Yashar Talebirad,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HASTE分层多智能体系统，通过三级知识作用域（全局、领域、竞赛特定）组织跨竞赛知识，实现从零开始到77.3%奖牌率，知识复用使精炼迭代减少52%、变更保留率从42%提升至85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML工程智能体每次竞赛都从零开始，浪费计算资源重复发现已有技术，缺乏跨竞赛知识复用机制。

Method: 构建分层多智能体系统（HASTE），将知识组织为全局、领域、竞赛特定三个作用域层级，每个层级匹配对应智能体；编排器协调领域专家并通过LLM驱动抽象促进层级间学习迁移。

Result: 分层加载在8个竞赛中达到100%奖牌率（平铺加载仅62.5%且消耗2倍输出token）；完整MLE-Bench Lite基准（22个Kaggle竞赛）达77.3%奖牌率；热启动减少52%精炼迭代，变更保留率从42%（低库存）升至85%（50+技能）。

Conclusion: 更好的知识组织可以部分替代模型强度和计算预算，是提升ML工程智能体效率的关键因素。

Abstract: ML engineering agents waste compute rediscovering known techniques because every competition is a cold start. We present HASTE, a hierarchical multi-agent system that organizes cross-competition knowledge into three scope tiers (global, domain, and competition-specific), each coupled to a matching agent level. An orchestrator coordinates domain specialists and promotes learning between tiers via LLM-driven abstraction. A controlled ablation provides evidence for scoped loading: holding a 159-skill inventory constant across 8 competitions, tiered loading achieves a 100% medal rate while flat loading reaches only 62.5%, the same medal rate as loading no skills, and consumes 2x the output tokens. On the full MLE-Bench Lite benchmark (22 Kaggle competitions), HASTE reaches a medal rate of 77.3% using Claude Sonnet 4.6 at 12h per competition. In a cold-start run, the system begins with no accumulated skills. In warm-start runs, it reloads skills learned from earlier competitions, using only global and domain-level skills for transfer across competitions. Warm starts use 52% fewer refinement iterations, and the fraction of proposed changes kept by the agent rises from 42% at low inventory to 85% once 50+ skills are available. These results suggest that better knowledge organization can partly substitute for model strength and compute budget in ML-engineering agents.

</details>


### [105] [RoPoLL: Robust Panel of LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30931)
*Anish Acharya,Kris W Pan,Brian Verkhovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化LLM陪审团为Huber污染模型，证明其存在无界偏差，提出使用几何中位数作为稳健聚合函数的RoPoLL方法，在13个模型、3个基准和4种腐败场景下，RoPoLL在各种有偏腐败类型上均优于传统PoLL，且3个法官的RoPoLL委员会（38B）在30%双峰随机腐败下超越Mistral-Large-3（675B）1.31倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Jury（PoLL）虽已成为单法官LLM评估的实用替代方案，但其统计行为尚不明确。作者旨在形式化分析其统计特性，并解决其在存在有偏腐败（如模式坍塌、谄媚、安全拒绝）时可能出现的无界偏差问题。

Method: 将LLM Jury形式化为Huber污染模型，证明PoLL在任意正污染率下存在无界偏差；将陪审团共识问题重构为经典稳健均值估计问题，提出RoPoLL，用几何中位数（GM）替代传统聚合函数；推导有限样本误差界与信息论极小化下界，指出两者在参数率上一致，在breakdown floor上存在sqrt(d)的统计-计算差距。

Result: 在13个开源法官模型、3个奖励模型基准、4种腐败机制（最高50%污染率）下，RoPoLL在每个有偏腐败类型上均优于PoLL：跨维度攻击提升约19%，重尾拜占庭对抗提升数个数量级；3法官RoPoLL（38B）在30%双峰随机腐败下以1.31倍性能超越Mistral-Large-3（675B），实现18倍参数优势。

Conclusion: RoPoLL通过替换聚合函数为几何中位数，无需调参即可以最优有限样本breakdown point 1/2解决PoLL在有偏腐败下的无界偏差问题；实验验证其有效性，且统计-计算差距表明多项式时间算法与不可解Tukey半空间中位数之间存在固有代价。

Abstract: The LLM Jury, a Panel of LLM Evaluators (PoLL) reporting consensus scores, has become a practical alternative to single-judge LLM evaluation, yet its statistical behavior remains poorly understood. We formalize the LLM Jury under the Huber contamination model and show that PoLL incurs unbounded bias
  under any positive contamination, regardless of jury size, whenever a single judge fails in a biased, LLM-typical way (mode collapse, sycophancy, safety refusal). Framing jury consensus as classical robust mean estimation, we propose RoPoLL (Robust Panel of LLM-as-Judge), which preserves the PoLL
  panel but replaces the aggregation function with a robust mean estimator, instantiated with the geometric median (GM): tuning-free, with the optimal finite-sample breakdown point 1/2. A finite-sample error bound and a matching information-theoretic minimax lower bound agree on the parametric rate
  sigma*sqrt(d/N) and differ on the breakdown floor by a factor of sqrt(d), a statistical-computational gap that polynomial-time RoPoLL pays relative to the intractable Tukey halfspace median. Across 13 open-weight judges (4B-675B), three reward-model benchmarks, and four corruption regimes at rates up
  to 50%, RoPoLL dominates PoLL on every biased corruption type: by about 19% on cross-dimensional attacks at matched compute, and by orders of magnitude on heavy-tailed Byzantine adversaries. A 3-judge RoPoLL committee at 38B beats Mistral-Large-3 (675B) by 1.31x on HelpSteer-2 under 30% bimodal-random
  corruption, an 18x parameter advantage at better accuracy; a Noisy-GT control confirms the premium is paid against biased contamination, not benign imprecision.

</details>


### [106] [AgRefactor: Self-Evolving Agentic Workflow for HLS Compatibility and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30949)
*Yang Zou,Zijian Ding,Yizhou Sun,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgRefactor是一个基于LLM的多智能体工作流，用于将软件重构为HLS兼容程序。它通过自进化记忆系统积累知识，结合自动化重构工具，在11个真实基准测试中的9个上优于或匹配现有方法，并实现了6.51x的加速比。系统完全自动化且开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将现实世界的软件转换为可综合的HLS代码仍然具有挑战性，因为语言支持受限且软硬件编程实践存在差距。现有自动化和基于LLM的重构方法缺乏灵活性、难以扩展且计算成本高。

Method: AgRefactor采用基于LLM的多智能体工作流，包含自进化记忆系统，可跨任务积累和检索事实性与策略性知识。同时集成了自动化重构工具，在LLM驱动的重写与高效的基于工具的转换之间取得平衡。

Result: 在11个具有挑战性的真实基准测试（比先前工作中最复杂的案例长5-10倍）中的9个上，AgRefactor优于或匹配最先进的自动化重构工具和基于相同框架的强LLM基线。在性能优化方面，与SoTA pragma调优工具相比实现了6.51x几何平均加速，与优化后的开源设计相比实现了1.20x加速，且额外资源开销不到20%。

Conclusion: AgRefactor是一种完全自动化、开源且高效的HLS重构方法，通过记忆系统和工具集成，在可扩展性、成本和性能方面均优于现有方案。

Abstract: High-Level Synthesis (HLS) provides a fast path from concepts to silicon, but converting real-world software into synthesizable HLS code remains challenging due to restrictive language support and the gap between software and hardware programming practices. Existing automated and LLM-based refactoring approaches partially address this problem, yet they often lack flexibility, struggle to scale, and incur high computational costs. We introduce AgRefactor, an LLM-based multi-agent workflow for refactoring software into HLS-compatible programs. AgRefactor incorporates a self-evolving memory system that accumulates and retrieves factual and strategic knowledge across tasks, improving robustness and efficiency on unseen programs. To reduce cost and enhance scalability, it integrates automated refactoring tools, enabling agents to balance LLM-driven rewrites with efficient tool-based transformations. On 9 out of 11 challenging real-world benchmarks, which are 5-10x longer than the most complex cases studied in prior work, AgRefactor outperforms or matches the state-of-the-art automated refactoring tool and a strong LLM-based baseline built on the same framework backbone. Further agentic performance optimization yields a 6.51x geometric mean speedup over the SoTA pragma tuning tool and a 1.20x speedup over optimized open-source designs with less than 20% extra resources. AgRefactor is fully-automated and open-sourced.

</details>


### [107] [Neuro-Bayesian-Symbolic Residual Attention Shallow Network: Explainable Deep Learning for Cybersecurity Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30953)
*Nicolaie Popescu-Bodorin,Madeleine Togher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种神经-贝叶斯-符号残差注意力浅层网络（NBS-RASN），通过12层80个可解释神经元实现可解释的网络安全风险评估，在20个开源项目中达到0.79-0.97置信度，证明浅层网络结合深度推理可超越黑盒深度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度模型为追求准确率牺牲了可解释性，而在高风险网络安全场景中，可解释性至关重要。研究旨在设计一种既能保持可解释性又不牺牲性能的混合架构。

Method: 构建80个可解释神经元、12层的浅层网络，将领域知识、因果推理和专家判断编码为可微分组件。设置门控机制强制执行五个认识论公理（精确性、因果性、可证伪性、透明性、完整性）。通过残差注意力和反馈循环模拟深度学习特性，最终输出可完全分解的确定性加权分数与专家调整值。

Result: 在覆盖OWASP Top 10:2025所有类别和语言风险等级的20个开源项目上验证，置信度分数达到0.79-0.97。可解释性通过架构设计保证，而非依赖训练算法。

Conclusion: 挑战了深度学习需要深层网络的假设，证明浅层网络结合深度推理在高风险网络安全领域可以超越不透明模型，打破准确率与可解释性之间的权衡。

Abstract: We introduce the Neuro-Bayesian-Symbolic Residual Attention Shallow Network (NBS-RASN), a hybrid neural architecture for explainable cybersecurity risk assessment in open-source ecosystems. Unlike deep models that trade interpretability for accuracy, our shallow network encodes domain knowledge, causal reasoning, and expert judgment as differentiable components. It uses 80 interpretable neurons across 12 layers, including a gatekeeper that enforces five epistemological axioms - precision, causality, falsifiability, transparency, and completeness - as hard constraints before propagation. Despite limited depth, the network exhibits deep-learning traits via residual attention and feedback loops, learning complex risk patterns without becoming a black box. It produces fully decomposable scores: a deterministic weighted component plus an expert adjustment, with each adjustment traceable to named amplifiers (blast radius, propagation speed, structural nature, default exposure, exploitation pattern, institutional criticality). We validate on 20 open-source projects covering all OWASP Top 10:2025 categories and language risk classes, achieving confidence scores of 0.79-0.97, and show that explainability is guaranteed by design, not by a training algorithm. This challenges the assumption that deep learning requires deep networks, proving that shallow networks with deep reasoning can outperform opaque models in high-stakes cybersecurity, where interpretability is essential.

</details>


### [108] [HyPOLE: Hyperproperty-Guided Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30966)
*Arshia Rafieioskouei,Tzu-Han Hsu,Matthew Lucas,Borzoo Bonakdarpour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyPOLE是一个利用超属性（HyperLTL时序逻辑）指导部分可观测多智能体强化学习（MARL）的框架，结合CTDE技术合成去中心化策略，在SMAC、MessySMAC和WildFire基准测试中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化规约在单智能体强化学习中已展现出数学严谨性、表达能力和策略定义能力等优势，但这些优势在多智能体强化学习（MARL）中尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出HyPOLE框架，利用超属性（HyperLTL时序逻辑）的表达能力指导部分可观测MARL的学习过程，并集成集中训练-分散执行（CTDE）技术来合成去中心化策略。

Result: 在SMAC、MessySMAC和WildFire三个基准测试上的评估表明，HyPOLE相较于基线方法具有明显优势。

Conclusion: HyPOLE成功将形式化规约（尤其是超属性）的优势引入MARL，证明了其在部分可观测场景下指导多智能体学习并合成有效去中心化策略的可行性。

Abstract: Formal specification is a powerful tool to guide the learning process and provides significant advantages over reward shaping: (1) mathematical rigor; (2) expressiveness to specify objectives and constraints, and (3) the ability to define tactics to achieve objectives. However, these benefits remain largely unexplored in the context of Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). This paper introduces HyPOLE, a novel framework for MARL under partial observability, where learning is guided by the expressive power of the so-called hyperproperties and, in particular, the temporal logic HyperLTL. We integrate Centralized Training for Decentralized Execution (CTDE) techniques with HyPOLE to synthesize decentralized policies, and our evaluation on SMAC, MessySMAC, and WildFire benchmark demonstrates clear advantages over baselines.

</details>


### [109] [AgentBound: Verifiable Behavioral Governance for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30970)
*Anuj Kaul,Qianlong Lan,Pranay Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI
